Local optimizer components:

A. Score and gradient functions:
- `fft_conv(a, b, N)`? For a=a self or cross. Use `np.fft.rfft(a,N)`, `irfft`, length 2n-1.
- For cross p*q, two FFTs. Could cache FFT of p during q inner iterations. For q update, p FFT fixed; need q FFT each. For p update, need p*p and gradient.
- To compute p*q, if p and q same, use A*A. If different, A_p * A_q.
- For weights `make_weights(c, beta)`: c length 2n-1 nonnegative. We can set p_k = exp(beta*(c_k - cmax)/cmax) / sum? Typically for max smoothing, weight high c more: use `exp(beta * (c - cmax)/cmax)` or `exp(beta*c/scale)`. We need for active set. Make weights sum1. For large beta, approaches active max. We can also cap. Use scale = max(c) maybe. Implementation stable.

B. Soft gradient descent in log-space:
For p, compute c, weights w, potential h_i = ∑ w_k p_{k-i} (for gradient of c_k?) We derived for self gradient: ∂ weighted sum of c_k wrt p_i = 2 ∑_{d=0}^{n-1} p_d w_{i+d} (if w length 2n-1). For general cross p*q linear in p: ∑ w_k (p*q)_k wrt p_i = ∑ w_k q_{k-i} = potential as above but with q not p. To update p for self, use h_self_i=∑ w_k p_{k-i}; grad_i = 2 h_self_i. For log space: grad_w = p*(2h - p·2h) = 2 p*(h - p·h). Factor irrelevant to Adam? It scales with h; we can just use h centered.
Compute h efficiently: `h = cross_potential(p, w, N)` using FFT with reversed weights as described. We can also compute via `np.convolve(p, w_rev, 'full')` for small but use FFT.
Potential function:
```
def potential_with_weights(a, w, N):
    # returns h length n: h_i = sum_{d=0}^{n-1} a_d w_{i+d}
    # using conv a * rev(w), slice [n-1:2n-1] reversed? Wait a*d*w_{i+d}, with a=p, w=weights.
    Aw = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w_rev??)
```
Let's derive for h_i = ∑ a_d w_{i+d}. Let L=2n-1, revW[k]=w[L-1-k]. Conv full (a * revW)_m = ∑_d a_d revW[m-d]=∑ a_d w[L-1-m+d]. Set L-1-m = i => m=L-1-i=2n-2-i. So h_i=conv[2n-2-i]. If compute `h_full = irfft(fft(a,N)*fft(revW,N),N)`, then h = h_full[2n-2 - arange(n)] = h_full[n-1:2n-1][::-1]? For i=0, index 2n-2; i=n-1 index n-1. Slice `h_full[n-1:2n-1]` gives indices n-1,...,2n-2; reverse gives 2n-2,...,n-1 correct. So `h = h_full[n-1:2n-1][::-1]`. Check n=2 earlier with a, w: h0=a0w0+a1w1 = conv[2], h1=a0w1+a1w2=conv[1]; slice [1:3]=[conv1,conv2], reverse =>[conv2,conv1] correct. Good.
This potential has sum? h_i maybe.

For update p via log gradient:
```
def log_gradient Potential h, p:
    center = p @ h
    grad_w = p * (h - center)
```
If we want minimize weighted sum of c, gradient is 2h, so minimize using negative gradient: w - lr * grad_w? Actually f approximation ∑w_k c_k = const + 2∑ h_i p_i? Moving p to reduce ∑h_i p_i => gradient ∂/∂p_i =2h_i. In log-space gradient 2p(h-mean). Adam does `w_new = w - lr*grad_w`, so w decreases where h high. That reduces h-weighted mass. Good.
We can use Adam with learning rate.

But pure gradient may stay in local; add line search and random restarts.

def try_update(base, cand, base_score, N, t_values):

       if sum<... continue

       if sc<best_score: best...
    return best,best_score,best_t,sc_best

```
def obj_grad_log(w, N, n, beta):
   p=softmax(w)
   c=fft_self(p,N)
   # smooth max approx: score_soft = 2n * (sum exp(beta c) / sum exp(beta c))? scale. For gradient, use weights proportional exp(beta c)/sum.
   wts=make_weights(c,beta)
   csoft = sum(c*wts)  # soft max
   F=2*n*csoft
   h=potential(p,wts,N)
   grad= p*(h - p@h)  # factor 2n? For F derivative =2n*∑w_k c_k; derivative wrt w =2n*2 p(h-mean)? So include factor 4n? L-BFGS-B scale OK. Use grad = 2.0 * p*(h - p@h) maybe.
   return F, grad
```
Use `scipy.optimize.minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxfun':..., 'maxls':...})`. It will call many times. But `w` unconstrained; softmax. Need avoid overflow: subtract max in softmax. Gradient exact for soft objective.

Let's formalize `smooth_score_and_grad(p, N, beta)` for soft objective:
- c length 2n-1.
- weights = exp(beta*c / cmax)/sum? If cmax=0, uniform. For smooth objective F=2n * (1/beta) logsumexp(beta*c). If use weights = exp(beta*(c-cmax))/sum, softmax = cmax + (1/beta) log(sum exp). If beta large. We can just use weights and weighted sum c wts as surrogate; gradient as above with factor. No need exact LSE.
`F = 2*n*sum(wts*c)`.
Gradient wrt w: `4*n*p*(h - p@h)`? Let's derive: F(p)=2n∑_k w_k p^T A_k p (with A maybe). ∂/∂p_i =2n∑ w_k 2 a_{k-i}p? =4n h_i. In log w gradient = p ∂F/∂p - (p·∂F/∂p) p? Since F(p(w)) with p=softmax(w), gradient wrt w_i = p_i (∂F/∂p_i - p·∂F/∂p). ∂F/∂p_i=4n h_i. So grad_w=4n p(h-havg). In Adam scaling irrelevant if divide LR; for L-BFGS include. We can use `grad=p*(h-havg)` and objective not match scale but okay.

If using L-BFGS-B, objective should be twice differentiable. Soft weighted sum with fixed beta is smooth. Good.

Manual Adam update uses gradient of weighted sum, similar.

def self_conv_fft(p, N):
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*len(p)-1]
    if c.size and np.min(c)<0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
    if not np.isfinite(c).all(): c=... 
    return c

def score_fft(p,N):
    c=self_conv_fft(p,N)
    if c.size==0: return inf, c
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    return (2*n*mx if n? Actually n=len(p))
```

`make_weights(c, beta)`: c clip nonneg. `s = np.max(c)`; if s<=0 return uniform. `x=(c-s)/max(s,1e-300)`; y=clip(beta*x, -745,0); e=exp(y); s=sum; if bad uniform.
This weights high c=1, low exp(-beta).

`conv_with_weights(a, w, N)`: returns h=a potential.
```
L=2*n-1

Wr = np.fft.rfft(wr,N)
A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)  # could cache a FFT
h = np.fft.irfft(A*Wr,N)
return h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

For cross potential with p weights where one sequence p and weights from p*q, same function with `a=p`.

```
def optimize_sequence(p0, n, deadline, seed):
    p=normalize(p0); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p,N)
    best_exact=...; exact_last=...
    w=logp_from_p(p); m=zeros; v=zeros; t_adam=0
    ap=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    beta=6.0; lr=0.05; stall=0; cycle=0
    while time<deadline:
       cycle+=1
       # if invalid reset
       c=self_conv_fft(p,N) (or update ap if p updated)
       score=2*n*max(c)
       if score<best_score: update best
       # maybe exact if n small and time
       # choose action based on cycle:
       if cycle % 1 == 0: do 1 gradient step with smaller lr? But each step computes c again; overhead. We can do batch.
```
Avoid repeated FFT each cycle too much. Structure cycle:
- For each cycle, compute c once from p and score.
- Decide method:
   * If cycle % 10 == 0: LP direction if time.
   * Else if cycle % 5 == 0: FW direction inner 3.
   * Else: one or two gradient Adam steps (using same c/weights). For gradient step, after computing p_new, need evaluate score (FFT) and accept.
- Update beta/lr adaptively.

Initialization candidates function:
```
def make_initial_candidates(n, parent, rng):
    cands=[]
    # parent resampled
    if parent is not None: cands.append(resample(parent,n))
    # uniform
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # powers
    x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    for alpha in [0.0,0.2,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
       p=np.power(x+1e-8, alpha)
       # reversed
       p2=np.power(1-x+1e-8, alpha)
       cands += [p, p*p? no normalize]
    # 2D powers: alpha low endpoint, beta high. Use grid alpha [0.2,0.5,0.8], beta [0,0.2,0.5,0.8]
    for a in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]:

Pseudo `run_pool`:
```
def run_pool(n, starts, deadline, seed, allow_lp=True):
    rng=...
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    states=[]
    for p in starts:
        p=normalize(p)
        score=score_fft(p,N)
        st={'p':p, 'w':logp_from_p(p), 'm':zeros,..., 'beta':rng.choice(...), 'lr':0.05, 'stall':0, 'score':score}
        states.append
    best_p = min by score p; best_score
    best_exact=exact...
    while time<deadline:
      # If only one state? 
      for idx,st in enumerate(states):
        if time>=deadline: break
        # natural diversity: each state has beta offset
        c,sc = compute self conv and score
        if sc<best_score: update best
        # exact throttle (run maybe every 45s total, not per state) 
        # Choose action
        cycle=st.get('cycle',0)+1
        if allow_lp and (cycle % 30 == 0) and time_remaining > 60 and random <0.3 and lp_solved<max:
            q = lp_direction...
            if q: accept line search
        elif cycle % 4 == 0:
            q=fw_direction(...)
            accept
        else:
            # gradient step (maybe 1 or 2)
            weights=make_weights(c, st['beta'])
            h=...
            cand, new_w,new_m,new_v,new_t = adam_step(...)
            accept line search small/medium
        st['cycle']=cycle
        # adapt beta/lr/stall
      # Periodic exact
    return best_p, best_score, best_exact
```

`resample`: For n=1 handle. Use `np.interp`.
```
def resample(p, m):
    p=normalize(p)
    if p.size==m: return p
    if p.size > m:
      # block average to reduce alias maybe then interp? Use interp of density
    xp=np.linspace(0,p.size-1,p.size)
    if m==1 return np.array([1.0])? p scalar sum1.
    xq=np.linspace(0,p.size-1,m)
    q=np.interp(xq,xp,p)
    q=np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s else uniform
    return q
```
Interpolation of density preserving sum approximately. Could integrate better using linear interpolation; sum mass approximated. Good.

Parent load returns normalized array of original length. If length >12000? We can still global best? We may not exact. But attempt primary downsample to 8192. Return original parent if not improved? `fw_direction` more detail:
Inputs: `p`, `A_p`, `N`, `n`, `beta_w`, `beta_q`, `inner`, `rng`.
```
q=p.copy()
logq=logp_from_p(q)
for s in range(inner):
   A_q=rfft(q,N)
   r=irfft(A_p*A_q,N)[:L]
   if bad: break
   weights=make_weights(r,beta_w)
   W_rev=rfft(weights[::-1],N)
   h=irfft(A_p*W_rev,N)[:L? potential length? h_full=irfft(A_p*W_rev,N)
   h=h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   # scale h:
   hmean=float(np.mean(h))
   hdev=h-hmean
   hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(hdev))) if >0
   if hmax<=0: break
   # update logq: move toward low h while keeping closeness
   # Option: logq = logp - beta_q * hdev/hmax? This loses memory.
   # Use exponential tilting: q ∝ exp(logp - eta*hdev/hmax)
   eta=beta_q
   logq_new = logp_from_p(p) - eta*(hdev/hmax)
   # blend with old logq to avoid jumps:
   logq = 0.3*logq + 0.7*logq_new
   q=softmax(logq)
return q
```
But using logp each inner; maybe `logp = logp_from_p(p)` precompute. The exact minimizer of linear objective for current weights would be q_i ∝ exp(-η h_i) (not tilted by p). Adding logp keeps close and smooth. This is like Fujimoto. `hdev` can be negative low; -eta negative? Wait if h high, hdev positive, logq_new = logp - eta*(hdev/hmax) lower, good. If h lower, higher. For very low h, hdev negative, logq_new > logp, good.

Let's implement `solve_lp_direction_small(q_current, n_lp, time_limit)`:
```
if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
try:
  A = np.empty((2*n_lp-1,n_lp+1), dtype=np.float64)
  A[:,n_lp] = -1.0
  for j in range(n_lp):
     A[j:j+n_lp, j] = q_current[:n_lp-j]
  c = np.zeros(n_lp+1); c[-1]=1.0
  Aeq = np.ones(n_lp+1); Aeq[-1]=0
  bounds = [(0,None)]*n_lp + [(0,None)]
  res = sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(2*n_lp-1), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':time_limit,'presolve':True})
  if res.status==0:
     x=res.x[:n_lp]
     x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0); x=np.maximum(x,0); s=x.sum(); if s>0 return x/s
except Exception: return None
```

We already have best_exact for each attempt. But `run_pool` might not exact best due time. In `propose_candidate`, before final, if time >10 and best length ≤12000, compute exact for best's FFT p (if not exact). Use `exact_score`. Then choose between exact known.
The choose global best:
```
best_norm=None; best_exact=INF; best_fft=INF
def better_if(p, fft_score, exact_score):
   if exact_score is not None and exact_score < best_exact: update
   else if exact unknown: compare FFT? But for final need exact. We can use FFT as proxy.
```
At end, if best exact known return that. If not, compute exact for top candidates (parent, p_best) within time.
Parent exact can be computed before attempts if n<=12000. If parent length >12000, maybe skip exact but still can use FFT.

Potential problem: `evaluate_sequence` max values if sum <0.01. Our final scale to sqrt(2n) ensures sum≥~64 for n≥2048, fine.

`logp_from_p(p)`:
```
p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.maximum(p,0)
s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return zeros
if p.size and np.max(p)<EPS: w=zeros else log(p/EPS)?
w=np.log(np.maximum(p,EPS)); w-=np.max(w); replace nonfinite 0; return w
```
If p has zeros, log -inf -> using EPS gives large negative but finite. Exponent may underflow but okay.

`softmax_log(w)`:
```
w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel(); w=np.where(np.isfinite,w,0); w-=max; e=np.exp(np.clip(w,-745,0)); s=e.sum(); if bad uniform; return e/s
```

`resample`:
```
def resample(p,m):
    p=normalize_p(p)
    if m<=0: return np.ones(1)
    if p.size==1: return np.ones(m)/m
    if p.size==m: return p
    xp=np.linspace(0.0,p.size-1,p.size)
    xq=np.linspace(0.0,p.size-1,m)
    q=np.interp(xq,xp,p)
    # linear interpolation may exceed? clip negative
    q=np.where(np.isfinite(q),q,0); q=np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum(); if s>0: q=q/s else q=np.ones(m)/m
    return q
```
If downsample, interpolation not mass-preserving exactly; normalize.

`average_downsample` maybe better for large to small: use blocks (a/m) average. But `np.interp` smooths; for parent length 4806 to 2048, interpolation okay. If we want preserve details, block average:
```
def downsample(p,m): if p.size>m: blocks=p.size//m; if blocks>1: q=p[:m*blocks].reshape(m,blocks).mean(axis=1); q/=sum; else interp
```
Could lose boundary. Use for initial candidates? Interpolation enough.

`make_candidate_family(n, rng)`:
Maybe split into `generate_candidates(n,parent,rng,limit)`.
```
def generate_candidates(n,parent,rng):
   cands=[]
   if parent is not None and parent.size:
       base=resample(parent,n); cands.append(base)
       for sig in (0.05,0.15,0.3,0.6):
           w=logp_from_p(base)+rng.normal(0,sig,n); cands.append(softmax_log(w))
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   x1=1.0-x
   cands.append(np.ones(n))
   eps=1e-9
   for alpha in (0.0,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.8,1.0,1.3):
       cands.append(np.power(x+eps, alpha))
       cands.append(np.power(x1+eps, alpha))
   for a in (0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8,1.2):
       for b in (0.0,0.2,0.4,0.6,0.9):
           cands.append(np.power(x+eps,a)*np.power(x1+eps,b))
           cands.append(np.power(x1+eps,a)*np.power(x+eps,b))
   # random log-sine
   for it in range(12):
       D=6
       coef=rng.normal(0,1.0,D)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,D)
       vals=np.empty(n)
       vals.fill(1.0)
       for m in range(D):
          vals += 0.45*coef[m]*np.cos(2*np.pi*(m+1)*x + ph[m])
       vals=np.maximum(vals,eps)
       cands.append(vals)
   # random exponential blocks
   for it in range(6):
       vals=rng.exponential(1.0,n)
       # smooth by local average?
       k=3
       padded=np.concatenate((vals[k:],vals,vals[:k]))
       vals=padded.reshape(n,3).mean(axis=1)? no lambda. Need block: `convolve` maybe.
       cands.append(vals)
   # normalize all
   filtered=[]
   for c in cands:
       q=normalize_p(c)
       if q.size==n: filtered.append(q)
   return filtered
```
For smoothing exponential, we can use `np.convolve(vals, np.ones(k)/k, mode='same')` but `np.convolve` O(n) if k small? yes. Use fixed.
Need avoid duplicate n=1.

`conv_weight_fft(A,p? no)`:
```
def potential_from_A(A,n,weights,N):
   wrev=weights[::-1]
   W=np.fft.rfft(wrev,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
   return h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
For cross weights r, weights length 2n-1.

`score_from_A(A,n,N)`:
```
c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
if min(c)<0: c=max
mx=max
return 2*n*mx
```
We can avoid recomputing c if we need weights? In state iteration:

score=2*n*max(c)
```

`adam_update(p, w_log, grad_w, m,v,adam_t, lr)`:
```
t=adam_t+1
b1=0.9;b2=0.999
m=m*b1+(1-b1)*grad_w
v=v*b2+(1-b2)*grad_w*grad_w
m_hat=m/(1-b1**t); v_hat=v/(1-b2**t)
dw=m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-12)
dw=where(finite,dw,0); dw=np.clip(dw,-20,20)
w_new=w_log - lr*dw
w_new=where(finite,w_new,0); w_new-=max
p_new=softmax_log(w_new)
return p_new,w_new,m,v,t
```
`grad_w` scale. If grad all zero, okay.

`fw_direction(p, A_p, n, N, beta_w, beta_q, inner, rng, logp=None)`:
```
if inner<=0: inner=1
q=p.copy()
logq=logp_from_p(q)
if logp is None: logp=logp_from_p(p)
for _ in range(inner):
   A_q=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
   r=np.fft.irfft(A_p*A_q,N)[:2*n-1]
   if bad: break
   wts=make_weights(r,beta_w)
   h=potential_from_A(A_p,n,wts,N)
   hmean=float(np.mean(h))
   hdev=h-hmean
   hscale=float(np.max(np.abs(hdev)))
   if hscale<1e-300: break
   hnorm=hdev/hscale
   # combined target q target: softmax(logp - eta*hnorm)
   target = logp - beta_q*hnorm
   # blend
   logq = 0.25*logq + 0.75*target
   q=softmax_log(logq)
return q
```

Finalize_sequence:
```
def finalize_sequence(p):
   p=normalize_p(p); n=p.size
   scale=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
   p=p*scale
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<0.01: p*=0.01/max(s,1e-300)
   return [float(x) for x in p]
```
Actually evaluate_sequence scale invariant; we could return p sum1 to reduce values. But previous sum sqrt(2n) gives max_b = score. Values for n=8192: sum ~128, average ~0.0156, max maybe ~? score target 1.5 => max_b ~1.5, individual coeff maybe <1? Fine. Both valid. Use sum sqrt(2n).

Build attempts:
```
def build_attempts(parent_len, has_parent):
  attempts=[]
  if has_parent and 1000<=parent_len<=10000:
     primary = parent_len if 1024<=parent_len<=8192 else (8192 if parent_len>8192 else 1024)
     attempts.append((primary,0.50))
  else:
     attempts.append((4096,0.50))
  if primary != 8192: attempts.append((8192,0.18))
  if primary != 4096: attempts.append((4096,0.12))
  if primary != 2048: attempts.append((2048,0.08))
  # If no 2048 maybe add
  # Normalize fracs to sum 0.88
```
But if parent_len=8192, attempts 8192 0.54? We can just fixed. Let's implement simple:

`make_weights(c,beta)`:
```
def make_weights(c, beta):
    n=len(c)
    if n==0: return np.array([0.5,0.5])? But c always nonempty.
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0); c=np.maximum(c,0)
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= EPS:
        return np.ones(n)/n
    x=(c-mx)/mx
    y=beta*x
    y=np.where(np.isfinite(y),y,-745.0)
    y=np.clip(y,-745.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(y)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=EPS:
        w=np.zeros(n); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return w
    return e/s
```
If beta inf? We cap. If beta 0? uniform? We set if beta<=0 uniform.

if beta <=0: return uniform
```

`score_fft(p,N)`:
```
def score_fft(p,N):
    p=normalize_p(p); n=p.size
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
        if float(np.min(c))<0: c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        mx=float(np.max(c))
    else: mx=0
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return INF
    return float(2.0*n*mx)
```

`conv_weight_fft(A, n, weights, N)`:
```
def potential_from_A(A,n,weights,N):
    weights=np.asarray(weights,float)
    L=2*n-1
    if weights.size != L:
       # truncate/pad
       w2=np.zeros(L); m=min(weights.size,L); w2[:m]=weights[:m]
       weights=w2
    wrev=np.ascontiguousarray(weights[::-1])
    W=np.fft.rfft(wrev,N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
    h=h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
    return h
```
If n=1, slice [0:1] length1 OK.

`adam_update` uses `np.zeros_like` with dtype. Ensure m/v same length.
```
def adam_update(p,w,grad_w,m,v,adam_t,lr):
    t=adam_t+1
    b1=0.9;b2=0.999
    grad_w=np.asarray(grad_w,float)
    m=m*b1+(1-b1)*grad_w
    v=v*b2+(1-b2)*grad_w*grad_w
    mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
    vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
    dw=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-12)
    dw=np.where(np.isfinite(dw),dw,0.0)
    dw=np.clip(dw,-20.0,20.0)
    w_new=w - float(lr)*dw
    w_new=np.where(np.isfinite(w_new),w_new,0.0)
    if w_new.size: w_new=w_new - float(np.max(w_new))
    p_new=softmax_log(w_new)
    return p_new,w_new,m,v,t
```
`b1**t` for large t fine (<2400 steps per state maybe much but okay). If t huge, 0.9**t underflows? t ~10000, 1e-435 underflow to0, denominator1; fine.

def try_update(base,cand,base_score,N,ts):
    base=np.asarray(base,float); cand=np.asarray(cand,float)
    if cand.size != base.size: cand=resample(cand, base.size)
    best_p=base.copy(); best_score=base_score; best_t=0.0
    for t in ts:
       t=float(t)
       if t <=0 or not np.isfinite(t): continue
       mix=(1.0-t)*base + t*cand
       mix=normalize_p(mix)
       sc=score_fft(mix,N)
       if sc < best_score:
          best_p=mix; best_score=sc; best_t=t
    return best_p,best_score,best_t
```
`normalize_p` makes copy. If base_score slightly NaN? It should not.

`fw_direction`:
```
def fw_direction(p,A_p,n,N,beta_w,beta_q,inner,rng):
    if p.size != n: ...
    logp=logp_from_p(p)
    q=p.copy()
    logq=logp.copy()
    inner=max(int(inner),0)
    for _ in range(inner):
       A_q=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
       r=np.fft.irfft(A_p*A_q,N)
       r=r[:2*n-1]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)): break
       minr=float(np.min(r))
       if minr < 0: r=np.maximum(r,0)
       weights=make_weights(r,beta_w)
       h=potential_from_A(A_p,n,weights,N)
       hmean=float(np.mean(h)) if h.size else 0.0
       hdev=h-hmean
       hscale=float(np.max(np.abs(hdev))) if hsize else 0
       if not finite hscale or hscale<1e-300: break
       hnorm=hdev/hscale
       # random slight jitter?
       if rng.random() <0.2:
          hnorm=hnorm + 0.05*rng.normal(size=n)
       target=logp - beta_q*hnorm
       # blend
       logq=0.3*logq + 0.7*target
       logq=np.where(np.isfinite(logq),logq,0)
       q=softmax_log(logq)
    return q
```

    else:
       lp_n = 512

    rem = min(rem, 8.0)
    small = resample(p, lp_n)
    qsmall = solve_lp_small(small, lp_n, rem)

def load_parent():

  if obj is None: return None
  try: arr=np.asarray(obj,float).ravel()
  except: return None

  s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: return None

If H(k)=X(k)X(k), H(-k)=X(-k)X(-k)=conj(X(k))^2. The conjugate of H(k) is conj(X(k))^2 = H(-k). Wait H(-k)=conj(X)^2, same as conjugate H(k) because H(k)=X^2, conj(H)=conj(X)^2. So H(-k)=conj(H(k)), Hermitian! Yes if same frequency. So A*A is Hermitian implicitly. Good. For cross convolution p*q, Xp*Xq is also Hermitian? H(-k)=conj(Xp)conj(Xq)=conj(H(k)). Let's write `run_pool` more concretely:

```
def run_pool(n,starts,deadline,seed,use_lp=True):
    start=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    L=2*n-1
    if not starts: starts=[np.ones(n)/n]
    states=[]
    best_p=None; best_score=INF
    # maybe max_states = min(3,len(starts)) but if starts 4, use 3? Let's use 3.
    max_states=3
    for i,p0 in enumerate(starts[:max_states]):
       p=normalize_p(p0)
       A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
       ... score
       beta=[5.0,10.0,20.0,40.0][i%4]
       lr=[0.08,0.05,0.04,0.02][i%4]
       states.append({})
    if len(states)==0: ...
    # best from states
    for st in states: if st['score']<best_score ...
    # exact best initialization
    exact_budget = max(2.0, min(30.0, 0.03*(deadline-start)))
    exact_time=0; last_exact=time.monotonic(); exact_count=0
    if n<=12000 and time<deadline-3:
       t0=time.monotonic(); sc=exact_score_norm(best_p); exact_time +=...; if finite...
    lp_calls=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       for idx in range(len(states)):
          if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
          st=states[idx]
          p=st['p']
          A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
          c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
          c=c[:L]
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
             reset_state(st,best_p,best_score); continue
          if np.min(c)<0: c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
          mx=float(np.max(c))
          score=2*n*mx
          st['score']=score
          if score < best_score:
             best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
          st['cycle']+=1
          cyc=st['cycle']
          improve=False
          remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
          # LP
          do_lp=False
          if use_lp and (not _LP_DISABLED) and _HAVE_SCIPY and remaining > 90:
             if lp_calls < 12 and (cyc % 40 == 0) and (rng.random() < 0.45):
                 do_lp=True
          if do_lp:
             q=lp_direction(p,n,remaining)
             if q is not None:
                 newp,sc2,tacc=try_update(p,q,score,N,(0.65,1.0,0.35,0.15))
                 if tacc>0 and sc2 < score:
                    set_state_p(st,newp,sc2); improve=True
                    lp_calls +=1
                 else:
                    lp_calls +=1
          elif cyc % 5 == 0 or ((st['stall'] > 15) and (cyc % 2 == 0)):
             beta_q = 8.0 + 2.0*min(20.0, st['stall']) + 0.02*cyc
             beta_q = min(80.0,beta_q)
             inner = 2 if n <= 4096 else 1
             q=fw_direction(p,A,n,N,st['beta'],beta_q,inner,rng)
             newp,sc2,tacc=try_update(p,q,score,N,(0.6,1.0,0.3,0.15))
             if tacc>0 and sc2 < score:
                set_state_p(st,newp,sc2); improve=True
          else:
             weights=make_weights(c,st['beta'])
             h=potential_from_A(A,n,weights,N)
             hmean=float(np.mean(h)) if h.size else 0.0
             grad=p*(h - hmean)  # since dot reasonable due sum p=1; maybe use dot
             # better use dot: grad=p*(h - float(np.dot(p,h)))
             dot=float(np.dot(p,h))
             grad=p*(h - dot)
             p_new,w_new,m_new,v_new,t_new=adam_update(p,st['w'],grad,st['m'],st['v'],st['adam_t'],st['lr'])
             newp,sc2,tacc=try_update(p,p_new,score,N,(1.0,0.5,0.25))
             if tacc>0 and sc2 < score:
                if tacc > 0.999:
                   st['p']=newp.copy(); st['score']=sc2; st['w']=w_new; st['m']=m_new; st['v']=v_new; st['adam_t']=t_new
                else:
                   set_state_p(st,newp,sc2)
                improve=True
          if improve:
             st['stall']=0
             st['lr']=min(0.25, st['lr']*1.04)
          else:
             st['stall'] += 1
             st['lr']=max(0.012, st['lr']*0.92)
          # beta adapt
          if st['stall'] > 10:
             st['beta']=min(180.0, st['beta'] + 1.5)
          elif st['stall'] <= 0:
             # allow beta to relax slowly after improvement? maybe keep
             pass
          if cyc % 80 == 0:
             st['beta']=min(140.0, st['beta']*1.06 + 0.3)
          # reset if stuck
          if st['stall'] > 60:
             if best_p is not None:
                if rng.random() < 0.55:
                   reset_state(st,best_p,best_score)
                else:
                   sig=float(rng.choice((0.08,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2)))
                   w=add noise log best
                   q=softmax_log(w)
                   sc=score_fft(q,N)
                   reset_state(st,q,sc)
                   st['stall']=0
          # If score much worse than best and many cycles, pull to best
          if st['stall'] > 30 and score > best_score + 0.05:
             if rng.random() < 0.3:
                reset_state(st,best_p,best_score)
       # exact validation after all states
       if n <= 12000 and exact_time < exact_budget and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
          if exact_count == 0 or time.monotonic()-last_exact > 40.0 or best_score < best_exact_score - 1e-5:
             t0=time.monotonic()
             sc=exact_score_norm(best_p)
             exact_time += time.monotonic()-t0
             exact_count +=1; last_exact=time.monotonic()
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score=sc; best_exact=best_p.copy()
    # final exact for each state top maybe
    if n <= 12000:
      for st in states:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline-1.0: break
        if exact_time > exact_budget*1.6: break
        if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) and st['score'] > best_exact_score + 0.05: continue
        t0=... exact st['p']
        update
    if best_exact is not None: return best_exact, best_exact_score, best_p, best_score
    return best_p, best_score, None, INF
```

Let's write carefully.

`score_fft(p,N)`:
```
p=normalize_p(p)
n=p.size
if n==0: return INF
A=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
c=c[:2*n-1]
if c.size:
    cmin=float(np.min(c))
    if cmin<0: c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(c))
else: mx=0.0
if not np.isfinite(mx): return INF
return float(2.0*float(n)*mx)
```
For p of length1: c length1, mx=p0^2, score=2*1*p0^2. But p sum1 => score2.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    evaluate_sequence(a) = 2 * len(a) * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

by optimizing the equivalent scale-invariant objective

    2 * n * max(convolve(p, p))

with sum(p)=1.  The search uses:
  - FFT-based smoothed-Linf / potential gradient steps in log-probability space,
  - Frank-Wolfe-style convex directions q minimizing max(p*q),
  - occasional exact LP solves of the same subproblem on a downsampled length,
  - multi-start / multi-length restarts,
  - throttled exact validation with np.convolve.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_HAVE_SCIPY = False
try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_LP_DISABLED = False
_EPS = 1e-300
_INF = 1e100


def _next_fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize_p(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    if s <= _EPS:
        return arr
    return arr / s


def _logp_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, _EPS)
    w = np.log(p)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    if w.size:
        w -= float(np.max(w))
    return w


def _softmax_log(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, -745.0, 0.0)
    if w.size:
        w -= float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    return e / s


def _resample(p, m):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if p.size == m:
        return p
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, float(p.size - 1), p.size)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, float(p.size - 1), m)
    q = np.interp(xq, xp, p)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        q = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        q = q / s
    return q


def _score_fft(p, N):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return _INF
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[: 2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return _INF
    if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return _INF
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * mx)


def _make_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= _EPS or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x = (c - mx) / mx
    y = float(beta) * x
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, -745.0)
    y = np.clip(y, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _potential_from_A(A, n, weights, N):
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if weights.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(weights.size, L)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = weights[:m]
        weights = tmp
    weights = np.where(np.isfinite(weights), weights, 0.0)
    wrev = np.ascontiguousarray(weights[::-1])
    W = np.fft.rfft(wrev, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    h = h[n - 1: 2 * n - 1][::-1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def _adam_update(p, w, grad_w, m, v, adam_t, lr):
    t = int(adam_t) + 1
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    grad_w = np.asarray(grad_w, dtype=np.float64)
    m = m * b1 + (1.0 - b1) * grad_w
    v = v * b2 + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_w * grad_w)
    mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
    vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
    dw = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
    dw = np.where(np.isfinite(dw), dw, 0.0)
    dw = np.clip(dw, -25.0, 25.0)
    w_new = w - float(lr) * dw
    w_new = np.where(np.isfinite(w_new), w_new, 0.0)
    if w_new.size:
        w_new -= float(np.max(w_new))
    p_new = _softmax_log(w_new)
    return p_new, w_new, m, v, t


def _try_update(base, cand, base_score, N, ts):
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if cand.size != base.size:
        cand = _resample(cand, base.size)
    best_p = base.copy()
    best_score = float(base_score)
    best_t = 0.0
    for tval in ts:
        tval = float(tval)
        if not np.isfinite(tval) or tval <= 0.0:
            continue
        mix = (1.0 - tval) * base + tval * cand
        mix = _normalize_p(mix)
        sc = _score_fft(mix, N)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_p = mix
            best_score = sc
            best_t = tval
    return best_p, best_score, best_t


def _fw_direction(p, A_p, n, N, beta_w, beta_q, inner, rng):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    if p.size != n:
        n = p.size
    inner = int(max(0, inner))
    logp = _logp_from_p(p)
    q = p.copy()
    logq = logp.copy()
    for _ in range(inner):
        A_q = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
        r = np.fft.irfft(A_p * A_q, N)
        r = r[: 2 * n - 1]
        if r.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
            break
        if float(np.min(r)) < 0.0:
            r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
        weights = _make_weights(r, beta_w)
        h = _potential_from_A(A_p, n, weights, N)
        if h.size == 0:
            break
        hmean = float(np.mean(h))
        hdev = h - hmean
        hscale = float(np.max(np.abs(hdev)))
        if not np.isfinite(hscale) or hscale <= _EPS:
            break
        hnorm = hdev / hscale
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            hnorm = hnorm + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
        target = logp - float(beta_q) * hnorm
        target = np.where(np.isfinite(target), target, 0.0)
        logq = 0.25 * logq + 0.75 * target
        logq = np.where(np.isfinite(logq), logq, 0.0)
        q = _softmax_log(logq)
    return q


def _solve_lp_small(q_current, n, time_limit):
    global _LP_DISABLED
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY or _LP_DISABLED:
        return None
    n = int(max(2, n))
    q_current = _normalize_p(q_current)
    if q_current.size != n:
        q_current = _resample(q_current, n)
    try:
        rows = 2 * n - 1
        A_ub = np.empty((rows, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        for j in range(n):
            A_ub[j:j + n, j] = q_current[: n - j]
        c_vec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_vec[-1] = 1.0
        A_eq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[-1] = 0.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
        options = {
            "presolve": True,
            "time_limit": float(time_limit),
            "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
            "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
        }
        try:
            res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
                c_vec,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options=options,
                disp=False,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
                c_vec,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
                disp=False,
            )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)[:n]
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            s = float(x.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > _EPS:
                return x / s
        return None
    except Exception:
        _LP_DISABLED = True
        return None


def _lp_direction(p, n, remaining_s):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY or _LP_DISABLED:
        return None
    rem = max(1.0, float(remaining_s) / 15.0)
    rem = min(rem, 6.0)
    if int(n) <= 1024:
        lp_n = max(256, int(n))
    elif int(n) <= 4096:
        lp_n = 512
    else:
        lp_n = 512
    small = _resample(p, lp_n)
    qsmall = _solve_lp_small(small, lp_n, rem)
    if qsmall is None:
        return None
    q = _resample(qsmall, n)
    return q


def _exact_score_norm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return _INF
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        return _INF
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return _INF
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return _INF
    val = float(2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else _INF


def _generate_candidates(n, parent, rng, max_cands=64):
    cands = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size:
        base = _resample(parent, n)
        cands.append(base)
        for sig in (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.70):
            w = _logp_from_p(base) + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n)
            cands.append(_softmax_log(w))
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return cands

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x1 = 1.0 - x
    eps = 1e-9

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3):
        cands.append(np.power(x + eps, alpha))
        cands.append(np.power(x1 + eps, alpha))
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return cands

    for a in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.2):
        for b in (0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.9):
            cands.append(np.power(x + eps, a) * np.power(x1 + eps, b))
            cands.append(np.power(x1 + eps, a) * np.power(x + eps, b))
            if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                return cands

    for gamma in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        vals = np.exp(gamma * (x - 0.5))
        cands.append(vals)
        cands.append(np.exp(gamma * (0.5 - x)))
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return cands

    for center in (0.35, 0.50, 0.65):
        for sig in (0.08, 0.18, 0.32):
            vals = np.exp(-((x - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            cands.append(vals)
            if len(cands) >= max_cands:
                return cands

    for _ in range(10):
        d = 6
        coef = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, d)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, d)
        vals = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(d):
            vals += 0.45 * float(coef[m]) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (m + 1) * x + phase[m])
        vals = np.maximum(vals, eps)
        cands.append(vals)
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return cands

    for _ in range(6):
        vals = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n))
        kern = np.ones(3, dtype=np.float64) / 3.0
        vals = np.convolve(vals, kern, mode="same")
        cands.append(vals)
        if len(cands) >= max_cands:
            return cands

    return cands


def _choose_starts(n, parent, rng, k):
    cands = _generate_candidates(n, parent, rng, max_cands=64)
    N = _next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    scored = []
    for idx, c in enumerate(cands):
        q = _normalize_p(c)
        sc = _score_fft(q, N)
        scored.append((float(sc), idx, q))
    if not scored:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]
    scores = np.array([s[0] for s in scored], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    starts = []
    for i in order:
        starts.append(scored[int(i)][2])
        if len(starts) >= int(k):
            break
    if not starts:
        starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    return starts


def _set_state_p(st, p, score):
    st["p"] = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    st["score"] = float(score)
    st["w"] = _logp_from_p(st["p"])
    st["m"] = np.zeros(st["p"].size, dtype=np.float64)
    st["v"] = np.zeros(st["p"].size, dtype=np.float64)
    st["adam_t"] = 0


def _reset_state(st, p, score):
    _set_state_p(st, p, score)
    st["stall"] = 0
    st["cycle"] = 0


def _run_pool(n, starts, deadline, seed, use_lp=True):
    start = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(max(2, n))
    N = _next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    max_states = 4 if n <= 2048 else 3
    states = []
    betas = (5.0, 12.0, 25.0, 60.0)
    lrs = (0.09, 0.06, 0.04, 0.02)

    best_p = None
    best_score = _INF

    for i, p0 in enumerate(starts[:max_states]):
        p = _normalize_p(p0)
        A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
        c = c[:L]
        if c.size and float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = float(2.0 * float(n) * mx) if np.isfinite(mx) else _INF
        st = {
            "p": p,
            "w": _logp_from_p(p),
            "m": np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64),
            "v": np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64),
            "adam_t": 0,
            "beta": float(betas[i % len(betas)]),
            "lr": float(lrs[i % len(lrs)]),
            "stall": 0,
            "cycle": 0,
            "score": sc,
        }
        states.append(st)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = _score_fft(best_p, N)

    exact_time = 0.0
    last_exact = time.monotonic()
    exact_count = 0
    best_exact = None
    best_exact_score = _INF
    exact_budget = max(2.0, min(25.0, 0.035 * max(0.0, deadline - start)))

    if n <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        sc = _exact_score_norm(best_p)
        exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0
        exact_count += 1
        last_exact = time.monotonic()
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = sc
            best_exact = best_p.copy()

    lp_calls = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        for _ in range(len(states)):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            st = states[0] if False else states[0]  # placeholder to avoid unused; actual below
            # NOTE: we iterate by index to modify list cleanly.
        for idx in range(len(states)):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            st = states[idx]
            p = st["p"]
            A = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
            c = c[:L]
            if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
                if best_p is not None:
                    _reset_state(st, best_p, best_score)
                continue
            if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            mx = float(np.max(c))
            score = float(2.0 * float(n) * mx)
            st["score"] = score
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()

            st["cycle"] = int(st["cycle"]) + 1
            cyc = int(st["cycle"])
            improve = False
            remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

            do_lp = False
            if use_lp and _HAVE_SCIPY and not _LP_DISABLED and remaining > 70.0:
                if lp_calls < 12 and (cyc % 40 == 0) and (rng.random() < 0.45):
                    do_lp = True

            if do_lp:
                q = _lp_direction(p, n, remaining)
                if q is not None:
                    newp, sc2, tacc = _try_update(p, q, score, N, (0.65, 1.0, 0.35, 0.15))
                    lp_calls += 1
                    if tacc > 0.0 and sc2 < score:
                        _set_state_p(st, newp, sc2)
                        improve = True
            elif (cyc % 5 == 0) or ((int(st["stall"]) > 15) and (cyc % 2 == 0)):
                beta_q = 8.0 + 2.0 * min(20.0, float(st["stall"])) + 0.02 * float(cyc)
                beta_q = min(80.0, beta_q)
                inner = 2 if n <= 4096 else 1
                q = _fw_direction(p, A, n, N, float(st["beta"]), beta_q, inner, rng)
                newp, sc2, tacc = _try_update(p, q, score, N, (0.6, 1.0, 0.3, 0.15))
                if tacc > 0.0 and sc2 < score:
                    _set_state_p(st, newp, sc2)
                    improve = True
            else:
                weights = _make_weights(c, float(st["beta"]))
                h = _potential_from_A(A, n, weights, N)
                dot = float(np.dot(p, h)) if h.size else 0.0
                grad = p * (h - dot)
                p_new, w_new, m_new, v_new, t_new = _adam_update(
                    p, st["w"], grad, st["m"], st["v"], int(st["adam_t"]), float(st["lr"])
                )
                newp, sc2, tacc = _try_update(p, p_new, score, N, (1.0, 0.5, 0.25))
                if tacc > 0.0 and sc2 < score:
                    if tacc > 0.995:
                        st["p"] = newp.copy()
                        st["score"] = sc2
                        st["w"] = w_new
                        st["m"] = m_new
                        st["v"] = v_new
                        st["adam_t"] = t_new
                    else:
                        _set_state_p(st, newp, sc2)
                    improve = True

            if improve:
                st["stall"] = 0
                st["lr"] = min(0.25, float(st["lr"]) * 1.04)
            else:
                st["stall"] = int(st["stall"]) + 1
                st["lr"] = max(0.012, float(st["lr"]) * 0.92)

            if int(st["stall"]) > 10:
                st["beta"] = min(180.0, float(st["beta"]) + 1.5)
            if cyc % 80 == 0:
                st["beta"] = min(140.0, float(st["beta"]) * 1.06 + 0.3)

            if int(st["stall"]) > 60 and best_p is not None:
                if rng.random() < 0.55:
                    _reset_state(st, best_p, best_score)
                else:
                    sig = float(rng.choice((0.08, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.20)))
                    w = _logp_from_p(best_p) + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n)
                    q = _softmax_log(w)
                    sc = _score_fft(q, N)
                    _reset_state(st, q, sc)

            if int(st["stall"]) > 30 and float(st["score"]) > best_score + 0.05 and best_p is not None:
                if rng.random() < 0.30:
                    _reset_state(st, best_p, best_score)

            if float(st["score"]) < best_score:
                best_score = float(st["score"])
                best_p = st["p"].copy()

        # Periodic exact validation of the current best.
        if n <= 12000 and exact_time < exact_budget and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.5:
            need = (
                exact_count == 0
                or time.monotonic() - last_exact > 35.0
                or best_score < best_exact_score - 1e-6
            )
            if need:
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                sc = _exact_score_norm(best_p)
                exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0
                exact_count += 1
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = sc
                    best_exact = best_p.copy()

    # Final exact checks for retained states.
    if n <= 12000:
        for st in states:
            if time.monotonic() + 1.0 > deadline:
                break
            if exact_time > exact_budget * 1.75:
                break
            if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) and float(st["score"]) > best_exact_score + 0.05:
                continue
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            sc = _exact_score_norm(st["p"])
            exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0
            exact_count += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = sc
                best_exact = st["p"].copy()

    if best_exact is not None:
        return best_exact, float(best_exact_score), best_p, float(best_score)
    return best_p, float(best_score), None, _INF


def _load_parent():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= _EPS:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _finalize_sequence(p):
    p = _normalize_p(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        p = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        n = p.size
    scale = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    p = p * scale
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        s = max(s, _EPS)
        p = p * (0.01 / s)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(max(budget, 120.0), 2388.0)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(30.0, budget - 30.0)
    total_search = max(60.0, deadline - start_time - 2.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = _load_parent()

    best_p = None
    best_fft = _INF
    best_exact = None
    best_exact_score = _INF

    if parent is not None:
        pp = _normalize_p(parent)
        pn = pp.size
        if pn <= 20000:
            N0 = _next_fast_len(3 * pn - 2)
            sc0 = _score_fft(pp, N0)
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_fft:
                best_fft = sc0
                best_p = pp.copy()
        if pn <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            scx = _exact_score_norm(pp)
            if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = scx
                best_exact = pp.copy()

    if parent is not None:
        pl = parent.size
    else:
        pl = 0

    if pl >= 512 and pl <= 8192:
        primary = int(pl)
    elif pl > 8192:
        primary = 8192
    else:
        primary = 4096

    if primary == 8192:
        attempts = [
            (8192, 0.46),
            (4096, 0.16),
            (2048, 0.10),
            (1024, 0.07),
            (512, 0.06),
        ]
    elif primary == 4096:
        attempts = [
            (4096, 0.46),
            (8192, 0.16),
            (2048, 0.09),
            (1024, 0.07),
            (512, 0.06),
        ]
    elif primary == 2048:
        attempts = [
            (2048, 0.44),
            (4096, 0.14),
            (8192, 0.12),
            (1024, 0.09),
            (512, 0.06),
        ]
    elif primary == 1024:
        attempts = [
            (1024, 0.42),
            (4096, 0.14),
            (8192, 0.14),
            (2048, 0.10),
            (512, 0.06),
        ]
    elif primary == 512:
        attempts = [
            (512, 0.40),
            (1024, 0.14),
            (4096, 0.14),
            (8192, 0.12),
            (2048, 0.08),
        ]
    else:
        attempts = [
            (primary, 0.42),
            (8192, 0.14),
            (4096, 0.10),
            (2048, 0.08),
            (1024, 0.07),
            (512, 0.05),
        ]

    frac_total = 0.0
    for _, f in attempts:
        frac_total += float(f)
    if frac_total <= 0.0:
        attempts = [(4096, 1.0)]
        frac_total = 1.0

    attempt_total = total_search * 0.90
    used_alloc = 0.0

    for idx, (n, frac) in enumerate(attempts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 25.0:
            break
        alloc = attempt_total * (float(frac) / frac_total)
        if alloc < 20.0 and idx > 0:
            continue
        attempt_deadline = min(deadline - 20.0, start_time + used_alloc + alloc)
        used_alloc += alloc
        if attempt_deadline < start_time + 15.0:
            break

        n_int = int(n)
        seed_i = int(seed) + 17 * idx + 3
        starts = _choose_starts(n_int, parent, rng, k=5)
        p_fft, sc_fft, p_exact, sc_exact = _run_pool(
            n_int, starts, attempt_deadline, seed_i, use_lp=True
        )

        if p_fft is not None and np.isfinite(sc_fft) and sc_fft < best_fft:
            best_fft = float(sc_fft)
            best_p = np.asarray(p_fft, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if p_exact is not None and np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = float(sc_exact)
            best_exact = np.asarray(p_exact, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        print(
            "attempt n=%d alloc=%.1fs fft_best=%.6f exact_best=%.6f"
            % (n_int, alloc, best_fft, best_exact_score if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) else float(sc_fft))
        )

    # Final polishing on the current best sequence.
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        n_pol = int(best_p.size)
        if n_pol < 512 or n_pol > 12000:
            n_pol = 4096
            best_p = _resample(best_p, n_pol)
        starts_pol = [best_p.copy()]
        w_pol = _logp_from_p(best_p) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=n_pol)
        starts_pol.append(_softmax_log(w_pol))
        polish_deadline = min(deadline - 10.0, start_time + total_search)
        if polish_deadline > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            p_fft, sc_fft, p_exact, sc_exact = _run_pool(
                n_pol, starts_pol, polish_deadline, int(seed) + 12345, use_lp=True
            )
            if p_fft is not None and np.isfinite(sc_fft) and sc_fft < best_fft:
                best_fft = float(sc_fft)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_fft, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            if p_exact is not None and np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = float(sc_exact)
                best_exact = np.asarray(p_exact, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    # Final exact validation for the best FFT candidate if exact is not already better.
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        if best_exact is None or (
            best_p.size <= 12000
            and (not np.isfinite(best_exact_score) or best_fft < best_exact_score - 1e-6)
        ):
            if best_p.size <= 12000:
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                scx = _exact_score_norm(best_p)
                if np.isfinite(scx):
                    if not np.isfinite(best_exact_score) or scx < best_exact_score:
                        best_exact_score = scx
                        best_exact = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if best_exact is not None:
        out = best_exact
    elif best_p is not None:
        out = best_p
    else:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    return _finalize_sequence(out)
```
